No, it hasn't wavered at all. I mean, for nearly two years now I’ve been completely sure that the contractor and some of the people on my block have been quietly working together to cheat us. They're keeping my kitchen half-wrong on purpose.

They are keeping my kitchen half-wrong on purpose. My husband keeps trying to show me receipts, and they all give me endless excuses, but... I know what they're doing. I just know it.\n\nDoctor: Would you say that the idea of your contractor and neighbors cheating you is something that most people would find hard to believe?\n\nPatient: Well, yes, I suppose they do find it hard to believe. My husband certainly doesn't believe it, and he tells me I'm being ridiculous. But just because other people don't see it doesn't mean it isn't happening. I mean, they aren't the ones looking at the shoddy work every day, seeing the little signs. So, yes, I guess most people wouldn't believe it, but that doesn't change the truth of what they're doing to me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any doubts about your belief that the contractor and your neighbors are cheating you?\n\nPatient: No, I... I really don't have any doubts. Like I mentioned a minute ago, this has been going on for two years now. I am absolutely certain the contractor and those neighbors are working together to cheat us. My husband can show me all the receipts he wants, and they can keep giving me excuse after excuse for why the kitchen is still left half-wrong, but... no. I know they are doing it on purpose. I don't doubt it at all.\n\nDoctor: Do you have any other thoughts or beliefs that you're absolutely certain of, but others might find hard to believe?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. It's really just this business with the kitchen. Everything else in my life is... well, it's fine, just normal day-to-day things. I don't have any other issues with anyone else, just this specific group who are messing with my house. Um, I haven't had any other... well, what you're calling unusual beliefs. Just this situation with the kitchen. No voices or... sounds or anything. Just normal things you'd hear around the house. My husband certainly doesn't believe it, and he tells me I'm being ridiculous. But just because other people don't see it doesn't mean it isn't happening. I mean, they aren't the ones looking at the shoddy work every day, seeing the little signs. So, yes, I guess most people wouldn't believe it, but that doesn't change the truth of what they're doing to me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any doubts about your belief that the contractor and your neighbors are cheating you?\n\nPatient: No, I... I really don't have any doubts. Like I mentioned a minute ago, this has been going on for two years now. I am absolutely certain the contractor and those neighbors are working together to cheat us. My husband can show me all the receipts he wants, and they can keep giving me excuse after excuse for why the kitchen is still left half-wrong, but... no. I know they are doing it on purpose. I don't doubt it at all.\n\nDoctor: Do you have any other thoughts or beliefs that you're absolutely certain of, but others might find hard to believe?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. It's really just this business with the kitchen. Everything else in my life is... well, it's fine, just normal day-to-day things. I don't have any other issues with anyone else, just this specific group who are messing with my house. So, no, nothing else comes to mind.\n\nDoctor: Has this belief about the contractor and your neighbors been the only unusual belief you've had over the past two years?\n\nPatient: Yes, that's really the only thing. Um, I haven't had any other... well, what you're calling unusual beliefs. Just this situation with the kitchen. My life is pretty quiet otherwise, just keeping up the house and, you know, normal retirement things. So, yes, it's just been this one issue with the contractor and the neighbors.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard any voices or sounds that seemed to come from outside your mind, commenting on this situation with the contractor and your neighbors?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't heard anything like that. No voices or... sounds or anything. Just normal things you'd hear around the house. I know what's going on because I can see it with my own eyes, not because anyone is talking to me in my head.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like you were being controlled by some kind of external force?\n\nPatient: No, nothing like that. I'm in control of myself. It's just... well, it's just the things I've told you about, with them coming into my home and moving things around. Nobody is controlling me or my thoughts.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been completely certain that the contractor and your neighbors are cheating you?\n\nPatient: Well, it's been about a year and nine months now. It all started back when the remodeling began on the kitchen and the garden. That's when things started getting disrupted around the house, and I realized what they were doing. So, yes, almost two years of this.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any other beliefs that you held onto for a long time, even if others didn't agree with you?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. I mean, I'm pretty practical. I just believe what I see happening right in front of me. I haven't had any other... unusual ideas or anything like that, if that's what you mean. Just the situation with the house.\n\nDoctor: Has your belief that the contractor and neighbors are cheating you ever changed or wavered in any way since it started?\n\nPatient: No, it hasn't wavered at all. I mean, for nearly two years now I’ve been completely sure that the contractor and some of the people on my block have been quietly working together to cheat us. They're keeping my kitchen half-wrong on purpose. \n\nMy husband... well, he tries to talk me out of it. He shows me receipts, and the contractor always has some excuse for why things aren't done right. But no matter how many receipts my husband shows me, or how many excuses they give, I know what I know. So no, my mind hasn't changed one bit.\n\nDoctor: Has this situation with the contractor and your neighbors caused any problems for you at work or with your relationships?\n\nPatient: Well, I'm retired, so I don't have to worry about work. But at home and around the neighborhood... yes, it has. I've completely stopped trusting certain neighbors, and I won't deal with those workers anymore. \n\nIt got to be too much, so my husband has had to take over most of the remodeling decisions lately. I just... I keep questioning what these people are really doing, and it causes arguments between us, so it's just easier if he handles talking to them now.\n\nDoctor: Have you changed your daily routines or behaviors in any significant way because of what you believe is happening with the contractor and your neighbors?\n\nPatient: Well, aside from what I just mentioned about the house, no, not really. Outside of dealing with the remodel, I still keep up with everything else. I go to my church gatherings, my book club... I still do my normal household routines and keep up with our family plans just the way I usually do. It's really just those specific people I avoid. Everything else in my day-to-day life is exactly the same.\n\nDoctor: Do you think there's any possibility, no matter how small, that you might be mistaken about the contractor and neighbors cheating you?\n\nPatient: No... no, I really don't. Like I said a minute ago, it's been nearly two years now, and I am just absolutely sure they are working together to cheat us and keep that kitchen half-wrong on purpose. I know my husband tries to prove otherwise with all those receipts, and those workers give him all sorts of excuses, but... well, I just know I'm right about what I see. So, no, there isn't any possibility I'm mistaken.\n\nDoctor: Has your belief about the contractor and neighbors cheating you ever been considered or considered as a possibility by anyone else, such as a friend or another family member?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I mean, my husband obviously doesn't believe it, like I told you. He thinks I'm making it all up. And... well, I haven't really brought it up to my friends at church or the book club. It's not the kind of thing you just chat about over coffee, you know? So, no, nobody else has said they think it's happening, but I also haven't really asked anyone else to look into it.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever been suspicious of other people or groups before, outside of this situation with the contractor and neighbors?\n\nPatient: No... no, I haven't. I mean, I've never really had a reason to be. I've always trusted people for the most part, you know? It's really just this whole mess with the house and those specific people. Before they started working on the kitchen, I never felt like anyone was trying to pull something on me or cheat me. So, no, I haven't had any issues like this with anyone else before.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Give candidates as ICD-10 codes, not disease names.\n- If the list changed from the previous candidates, say in \"note\" what this turn added or ruled out.
